## 🔁 STALE-CLIENT FIX + plan docs pinned to TOP of Ideas/Current · live `index-Cxq2W_dD.js` (site-deploy) · commit `b692bc0` (2026-06-28)
**Halli couldn't find the two plan entries — his installed PWA/browser was stuck on an OLD cached bundle.** Diagnosed + fixed:
- **The server was NOT stale** — the served bundle already contained both entries + docs (grep-verified the live JS). The stickiness is the **installed PWA**: there is **NO service worker** (only `manifest.json`), and `index.html` is served fresh (Cloudflare `DYNAMIC`), so normal reloads self-heal but an installed PWA clings to its cached start page across deploys.
- **Fix shipped:** (1) `src/main.jsx` now has a **loop-safe, SW-free build guard** — re-fetches `index.html` (no-store) on load + on PWA foreground; if a newer `index-*.js` is referenced it reloads ONCE per session (`sessionStorage` `fw_build_reloaded` → can't loop). Prevents recurrence going forward. ## 🐞 FIX — LIVE Journal rendered SHRUNKEN/zoomed-out · SHIPPED LIVE · 2026-06-22 · live `index-DvURn4_i.js`
**Halli: the live Journal redesign renders shrunken/zoomed-out with large empty margins (not filling the phone).** Root cause: **`JournalHub.jsx` root div was the ONLY redesigned page missing `overflowX: hidden`** (TodayDemo6 root = `overflow:hidden`; ProfileClipboardDemo = `overflowX:"clip"`). Without it, the decorative `VineMotifV2` vines (`position:absolute; right:-26`) + the fixed nav/jump-bar overflowed the page → on mobile the layout viewport widened and the browser **zoomed the whole page out to fit** = the shrunken look. **Fix (commit `7b7338c`, `JournalHub.jsx`):** added `overflowX: "hidden"` to the page root (+ explicit `width:"100%"` on the standalone SummaryCard — a no-op since snap=false already yields 100%, kept as intent marker). Did NOT touch flora.jsx/BRAND_IDENTITY.md.
- **VERIFICATION (Claude_Preview MCP @375px mobile, local dist = identical built code):** measured the page root with vs without the fix — **without `overflowX:hidden`: `document.scrollWidth = 459` (84px overflow past the 375 viewport → ~82% shrink-to-fit = the bug); with the fix: `scrollWidth = 375` (== viewport, zero overflow).** Content fills correctly: summary card spans left 16 → right 359 (343px, normal 16px gutters); slider track 14→361. **Cross-page compare:** /Today @390 = day-card left 16 → right 374 (358px, same 16px gutters) — Journal now fills identically to the other pages. lint 0 / vite build 0. **Live:** femwells.com/Journal HTTP 200; deployed bundle `index-DvURn4_i.js` (via `npx base44 site deploy -y`).
- **⚠️ screenshot NOT captured:** the Claude_Preview screenshot renderer timed out 4× this session (DOM eval works fine; renderer subsystem unresponsive) AND the Playwright MCP browser profile is locked by a concurrent session ("Browser is already in use"). Verification is via the direct width/overflow measurements above (the definitive evidence for a scale/overflow bug). A literal phone screenshot can be grabbed when a browser frees.
- **✅ done:** diagnosed (root overflow inconsistency) · fixed · lint/build · commit+push+deploy · @375px measurement proof (459→375) + cross-page width compare. **⏭ owed:** a phone-width screenshot once the preview screenshot renderer / Playwright frees.

- **MOTION smoother + the CLUNK ROOT CAUSE:** the slide had been animating `left` / a rAF FLIP — both run on the MAIN THREAD, so when a heavy keep-alive page mounts on navigation the animation **froze mid-slide** (that was the "clunky/stuck" feel) AND a CSS `translateX(100%)` mis-resolved (263px not 66 → wrong slot). **Final fix = fully DECLARATIVE GPU transform:** measure only the column width (one number, ResizeObserver), then React sets `transform: translateX(activeIndex × colW)px` inline; the compositor runs the **180ms easeOutCubic** slide — never freezes during a page mount, and the pill is deterministic from activeIndex so it always lands on the right slot. Removed all the px-measure/RO-resnap/left/FLIP machinery (net −90 lines).
- **LIVE QA (Playwright 390px, served `index-DUPyhclB.js`):** ✅ clean single-nav alignment **off 0** (translateX(0) on Today, aria-current matches); ✅ size 97%×97%; ✅ transition `transform 0.18s`; ✅ Jess open `navVisibleOnTop:true` + `pillOnJess:true`; ✅ Menu open `navVisibleOnTop:true`. **Caveats:** rapid-fire loop measurements were flaky (this headless session's `apps/null` retry storms + heavy keep-alive mounts delay React's commit past the wait — a real authed device settles <300ms); Menu-under-scroll-lock pill read off (desktop-Chromium scrollbar width-shift; phones use overlay scrollbars → no shift). ## ✅ PERF — KEEP-ALIVE nav tabs → instant page switching (IG-style) · SHIPPED + LIVE QA PASS · 2026-06-21 · live `index-BA30MVq9.js`
**Halli: page nav doesn't feel instant like IG.** Root cause: every route remounts the page AND all 56 pages fetch raw via `base44.entities` on mount (0 use react-query) → a full reload on every nav. **Fix (commit `1e9806b`, `src/App.jsx`):** KEEP-ALIVE the 3 nav-tab pages (Today/Lifestyle/Profile) — mounted once then **visibility-toggled (`display`)** instead of unmounted, so returning is INSTANT (no remount, no refetch, scroll preserved). Also **warmed during idle** (`requestIdleCallback` after first paint) so even the first tap is instant — this is the "prefetch on intent" ask, done proactively for all tabs. Non-tab routes still mount/unmount through the animated cross-fade (gated off while on a keep-alive tab → active tab never double-mounts). Persistent shell + liquid pill unchanged.
- **TIMING (live, Playwright 390px) — BEFORE vs AFTER:** tab switch BEFORE `~188–223ms` (remount+refetch) → AFTER **`~42–52ms`** (render + display toggle, ~next-frame). ~4–5× faster, and on a real device the gap is bigger (baseline refetches; keep-alive doesn't).
- **LIVE QA (served `index-BA30MVq9.js`):** ✅ app loads (no white-screen); ✅ exactly ONE keep-alive tab visible at a time (Today block / Lifestyle+Profile `display:none`, idle-warmed) — no double-mount; ✅ Today renders clean (screenshot `keepalive-today.png`). **NO REGRESSION to nav fixes:** pill full-height 52 + centered on Lifestyle; Jess open → `navVisibleOnTop:true` + `pillOnJess:true`; Menu open → `navVisibleOnTop:true` + `pillOnMenu:true`. Console = benign headless `apps/null` 404s only.

- **(1) Bigger active pill:** small per-icon wax square → ONE pill that FILLS the item (50px tall × item-width − 6px inset) behind icon+label; warm cream→gold wax fill, lit top, gold ring.
- **(2) Liquid migration (Web Animations API, compositor-only transform → 60fps):** pill MIGRATES old→tapped item, squash-stretching mid-travel — "liquid in a tube". **MOTION SPEC (for brand-bible motion section — NOT edited here, owner folds in):** easing `cubic-bezier(.5,1.18,.35,1)`; duration `clamp(360, 300 + dist*0.55, 560)ms`; squash-stretch `scaleX→1.30 / scaleY→0.90` at midpoint (volume-preserving), `1×1` at rest; cancels in-flight anims on rapid taps; re-measures on resize; reduced-motion → instant settle. Chose migrating-pill+squash-stretch over an SVG metaball/goo filter (goo blurs text + GPU cost; single pill has no 2nd blob to merge — the stretch sells liquid at zero filter cost).
- **(3) Page transitions — ROOT CAUSE FOUND:** `App.jsx` rendered the WHOLE `<Layout>` INSIDE the per-route keyed `motion.div` under `AnimatePresence mode="popLayout"` → every nav unmounted+remounted the ENTIRE shell (two layouts/navs co-existed per transition). Pages are STATICALLY imported (no lazy chunks) → it was **shell-remount churn, not route-load latency** (big ~5.8MB initial bundle = separate lazy-load opportunity, out of scope). **Fix:** hoisted ONE persistent `<LayoutWrapper>` ABOVE the transition; only the PAGE cross-fades inside the keyed `motion.div` (opacity + 6px settle, exit 0.12s / enter 0.22s). Nav/shell never remount. `MotionConfig reducedMotion="user"` → opacity cross-fade fallback. Bare routes (PartnerView, admin/*) early-return no-shell (preserved). `currentPageName` derived from pathname + alias map.
- **VERIFICATION:** Build clean (vite exit 0). Live bundle string-check PASS (pill easing, WAAPI scaleX/scaleY, `MotionConfig reducedMotion`, `PAGE_ALIASES`, `Open Jess`); shell HTML serves. Hooks-order checked safe (all hooks above the bare-route early returns). **⚠️ LIVE RUNTIME/visual QA NOT done — Chrome-extension MCP disconnected AND Playwright MCP locked by a concurrent session this whole window; no local headless lib. Outstanding: real-device confirm — no white-screen, nav renders, pill MIGRATES (not jumps), smooth page swaps, reduced-motion cross-fade, 0 console errors. Re-run when a browser frees.**

## 🐝🌼 BRAINSTORM — CONNECTION · DAYS-OFF ACTIVITIES · TINY MISSIONS ("No Garden Grows Alone") · DELIVERED + wired to IDEAS + live, awaiting Halli (2026-06-20) · live `index-BMEtbQXO.js`
**Halli: women feel lonely / swamped by work — give them (a) gentle ways to CONNECT with other women, (b) days-off ACTIVITIES found FOR them, (c) TINY MISSIONS that fulfil. Deep brainstorm + research, no code feature built.** (commit `bd42f99`, pushed, deployed.)
- **Unifying frame:** *"No garden grows alone."* Connection = **the bees** (pollination between gardens; Community is already sage+bee) · days off = **a day to wander** (the garden finds her ONE outing) · tiny missions = **seeds & waterings** (small acts grow her garden / earn a pollinator). All on the v4 living-ecosystem grammar (lifecycle · omens · soulful voice · cards · quick-action popup). Strips nothing — builds on existing Community (anon/k-anon rooms, Circles, Echo Wall, QOTD, Garden-of-Gardens), Sealed Letters, NurtureGarden signals, HabitLogs/PlannerItems, LifestyleItems.
- **CONNECTION = a SAFETY-FIRST ladder (no open matchmaking assumed):** **Tier 0** (ship-first, lightest legal duty) = "someone like you" k-anon *resonance* (no contact) + season/domain async prompt rooms + living Garden-of-Gardens + "tell the garden your news"; **Tier 1** (needs build+moderation+your nod) = moderated async **sealed-letter pen-pal** between strangers (the Slowly model, no DMs/profiles/locale); **Tier 2** (open matching/DMs/locale) = NOT recommended, needs legal counsel. **DAYS OFF:** ONE curated pick (choice-overload), surfaced as a Lifestyle "A day for you" lane + Today evening-before nudge; solo+social, whole-life not chores. **TINY MISSIONS:** one optional/day, static library + daily-seed rotation (like floraOmen.js), no-guilt (Finch-not-Forest), completion → quick-action popup → garden growth / earned pollinator / rare-bloom milestone.
- **Research-backed (`workspace/CONNECTION_ACTIVITIES_MISSIONS_RESEARCH_2026-06-20.md`, every claim a verified URL):** async beats matching (Hey!VINA dead, Bumble killed BFF-in-app, Slowly won; friendship=~50/90/200 *leisure* hrs not matching), choice-overload jam study (24→3% vs 6→30%), nature-dose ≥120min/wk (White 2019), awe walks (UCSF), kindness boosts the GIVER bunched+varied (Lyubomirsky), streak-guilt backfires/SDT, UK women lonelier (7.67% vs 6.33%) + motherhood spike. **HONEST FLAG:** phase/mood-matched activity has NO rigorous evidence → gentle tint only, never clinical.
- **LEGAL FLAGS for Halli (gated territory, NOT signed off):** UK Online Safety Act 2023 (no size exemption; duties scale with functionality — no profiles/no stranger-DMs/no locale = de-risking) + ICO Children's Code (geoloc-off, no nudging) + men-posing-as-women risk. The safe path = the warm path. **4 decisions surfaced** (connection depth · moderation model · location ever · age gate).

Gold hairline kept. **Botanical brand touch:** a faint `CornerSprig` (gold, opacity .12) tucked into each rounded end, clipped to the pill, `z-index:-1` behind the row. **Fallback:** `useState` feature-detects `CSS.supports('backdrop-filter'…)`; when unsupported → opaque `--surface` cream (never see-through/illegible).

## ✅ JESS — LIVE ASSISTANT NOW ACTUALLY WORKS + INLINE ASK-JESS (SHIPPED + live-QA PASS · 2026-06-19) · live bundle `index-vjHWl8tB.js`
**Problem (Halli):** the live `/Assistant` (Jess) page was a dead stub ("Jess / Assistant is open / Back to Today") and the "Ask Jess" actions (Health corner + others) LINKED OUT to that dead page instead of answering in context.
**Backend (intended + confirmed live):** Jess rides the EXISTING `base44.agents` agent `personal_assistant` (the agents API — NOT a base44 *function*, so the 50-fn cap doesn't apply; no model key needed). `JessDemoPanel` already drives it. Verdict = option (a) pure UI wiring; **no gated decision required.** Verified live: sent "Why am I tired today" → personalised cycle-aware reply; seeded "magnesium and sleep" → real answer + MHRA note.
**Root causes found:** (1) `/Assistant` was a hollow stub that only dispatched `fw_open_assistant` and relied on the global overlay opening — but on a direct/hard load the page's mount effect fired BEFORE Layout attached its listener (effects run child-first), so the event was missed → user stranded on the stub. (2) The Ask-Jess seed prompt was dropped at THREE points: Layout read `e.detail.prompt` not `.initialPrompt`; `AssistantOverlay` ignored `initialPrompt`; `JessDemoPanel` never accepted/read it (the `jess_open_chat`/`jess_chat_prompt` sessionStorage keys JessPatternNudge set were never read anywhere). (3) Health `askJess` + JessPatternNudge `tellJess` did `navigate("/Assistant")` / `window.location.href` → bounced to the dead page.
**Fixes shipped (commits `225635e`, `6b00d8f`, `edc801a`):**
- `/Assistant` (`src/pages/Assistant.jsx`) now renders the real `JessDemoPanel` directly in a fixed box above the bottom nav — **Jess answers on the page**; seeded from sessionStorage; no event race.
- Threaded `initialPrompt` through `Layout` (reads `.prompt || .initialPrompt`) → `AssistantOverlay` → `JessDemoPanel`, which **auto-sends it once** as the user's first turn (via a `sendUserTextRef` + stable deps so profile/context hydration can't cancel the send timer — that flakiness was caught + fixed in live QA).
- Opener suppressed when a seed is present (gated the 1.4s opener effect on `initialPrompt`) so it can't race/overwrite the seeded Q&A.
- **Health corner Ask-Jess + JessPatternNudge "Tell Jess" now open the INLINE overlay in context** (dispatch `fw_open_assistant` with `initialPrompt`), no navigation. SavedItemCard/AICoachTab still link to `/Assistant` — fine now that the page works.
- **NOT changed:** the `personal_assistant` agent, any function, the JessDemoPanel chat engine/brand palette (legacy `C` tokens kept — repainting is out of scope + risky; noted as a future brand pass).
**Live QA (Chrome MCP, test user @ 390px, bundle `index-vjHWl8tB.js`):** `/Assistant` renders real panel (stub gone), input "Message Jess…" present, **0 overflow, 0 console errors**; live agent answered a free-text question. Inline flow on `/Health`: dispatch → overlay opens **inline (URL stays /Health)**, seed auto-sends, **opener NOT shown**, Jess answered ("…magnesium and sleep…"), 0 overflow. Files: `src/Layout.jsx`, `src/components/assistant/AssistantOverlay.jsx`, `src/components/assistant/JessDemoPanel.jsx`, `src/components/jess/JessPatternNudge.jsx`, `src/pages/Assistant.jsx`, `src/pages/Health.jsx`. Bundle progression `zsTTxkLW → DtMvC59J → vjHWl8tB`.

## 🚧 BRAND P2 — CORRECTIVE SWEEP (IN PROGRESS · started 2026-06-18, after Halli's on-PHONE review found cross-page regressions)
**Why:** the Brand-P2 visual rollout shipped, but Halli reviewed on his ACTUAL PHONE and the "done" pages are NOT clean. Four cross-page problems: (1) **type not unified** to §1 across pages (fonts/sizes fighting); (2) **type too small on-device** + a **QA calibration bug** — a 390px DESKTOP window renders CSS px ~40% larger than a physical phone, so my QA passed pages that are tiny in the hand; (3) **off-brand dark header slabs** (Lifestyle masthead was a dark `T.dusk` block — brand bg is cream); (4) **jump-to switcher missing** on multi-layer pages (Today, Lifestyle, …). Corrective sweep runs FIRST, then resume any remaining polish.

### >>> ROUTING TABLE — EDIT THE ROUTED COMPONENT, NOT THE SAME-NAMED FILE (learned the hard way) <<<
`pages.config.js` maps routes to components. The live pages are the **Hub** variants; the same-named files are UNROUTED fallbacks. Confirm here before editing:
- `/Journal` → **`src/pages/JournalHub.jsx`** (NOT Journal.jsx = `/JournalClassic`, unrouted fallback)
- `/Community` → **`src/pages/CommunityHub.jsx`** (NOT Community.jsx = `/CommunityClassic`)
- `/Nutrition` → **`src/pages/NutritionHub.jsx`** (NOT Nutrition.jsx)
- `/Lifestyle` → `Lifestyle.jsx` · `/Planner` → `Planner.jsx` · `/Profile` → `Profile.jsx` · `/ProgramsHub` → `ProgramsHub.jsx` · `/Garden` → `Garden.jsx` · `/Today` → `TodayDemo6.jsx`
- `/Health` → **`src/pages/Health.jsx`** and `/DoctorExport` → **`src/pages/DoctorExport.jsx`** — these are routed DIRECTLY in `src/App.jsx` (lines ~208/218), NOT via `pages.config.js`. (`/SkinHair`, `/LifeStageCare`, `/HealthDashboard` all `Navigate` → `/Health`; `/CareBridge` → `/DoctorExport`.) Jess is a FAB/overlay, not a page route.
- **SHARED CARD:** all three hubs render `SurfaceCard` from `src/components/nutrition/hub/HubShell.jsx` — branding it once frames Journal+Nutrition+Community cards together (done).

### Shared helpers (promoted to `src/components/brand/flora.jsx`)
`CardFrame({variant,color,opacity,size})` = the 4-corner sprig/carved frame (overlay inside a `position:relative;overflow:hidden` card; set content `position:relative` so it paints above corners). `clampLines(n)` = wrap+N-line-clamp safe-text. Reuse these on every page.

## ✅ FLORA — COLOURWAY MULTIPLIER (parameterised colour variants → billions of gardens) — SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-17) — live `index-CAXTF7ZJ.js` · commit `a85c9fa` · PREVIEW/CONCEPT ONLY
- Halli: not enough variety — wants more flower TYPES + COLOUR VARIANTS per flower, and the SAME for creatures, for a much bigger combinatorial space. Researched flower/butterfly **colour** symbolism (colour changes meaning). Kept everything prior (no regression).
- **COLOURWAY SYSTEM (the multiplier):** `COLORWAYS` = **9 on-brand palettes** each a documented colour-meaning (crimson=love/remembrance · blush=grace · gold=joy · sage=renewal · plum=dignity · lavender=serenity · cream=purity · coral=warmth · sky=trust). `FlowerGlyph`/`Butterfly`/`Creature` **parameterised** by `color`/`color2`/`accent` (+ butterfly `pattern`) → one drawn shape = many variants, no hand-drawing.
- **MORE TYPES:** +6 flowers (tulip/rose/iris/daffodil/bluebell/carnation) → **18 flower types**; +2 creatures (snail/firefly) + colour params on bee/ladybird → **8 creature types**; butterfly **5 wing-patterns** (spots/bands/tips/eyes/plain).
- **COUNTS / PERMUTATIONS:** 18 flowers × 9 colourways = **162 flower variants**; butterflies 5×9 = **45**; full creature set **100+**; garden permutation space **≈ 1.4 billion** (BRAND_FLORA §6.4/§7.1). Colour is meaningful (white poppy ≠ crimson poppy).
- **SHOWCASE (`/BrandCraftSample`):** "one flower → 9 colourways" swatch grid (camellia, each labelled with colour-meaning), "one butterfly → many wing-patterns" row, 18-flower + 8-creature library rows, and **six distinct user gardens** (Maya/Priya/Noor/Ada/Lena/Sol — visibly different). `MiniGarden` now seeds species × colourway × creature × pattern.
- **FINGERPRINT enlarged:** uniqueness draws from species × colourway × creature × creature-colourway × wing-pattern × plant × combination × accent — deterministic (hash of userId+stage+phase+tended+earned), same on every device.
- **DOCS:** BRAND_FLORA §6.4 (colour language) + §7.1 (permutation space) + sources; Founders "Flora & Meaning" tab (+colour multiplier, counts, permutation space); BRAND_IDENTITY §5.1 folded in.

The `/BrandCraftSample` perf card animates **8 elevated blooms** + a live fps meter for on-device readout.

Card dimensions never change between states. Uniform size + cream colour; vertical list of independent rows; horizontal within-row slide — kept.
- Geometric note: a true 100%-row-width card can't show a side peek (no room); 80% is the largest size that still leaves the ~20% peek strip, and it matches the earlier big-rows look.

## 📖🏠 READER CHAPTER-MAP + REFLECT + CALM SCROLL · TODAY WATER/MEAL/CYCLE/CALENDAR/STACK — SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-16) — live `index-Bw-a-Veo.js` · commit `451c918`
- **8 Halli-flagged fixes; read BRAND_IDENTITY gate; all live-verified at 390px.**
- **(1) CHAPTER ≠ PAGE — ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED.** The earlier "fix" only collapsed the TOC; the erratic labels (Ch4 ×30 pages → 5 → 6) were a READER race: `flipForward` had no in-progress guard (rapid taps queued multiple advances during the 600ms anim) AND it advanced off a freshly-entered chapter whose page-count was still unmeasured (defaults to 1 → skip). Fix: flip debounce (`flipState.phase==="flipping"` → ignore) + **never advance off a chapter until `measuredPages` is known**. Diagnosed by fetching the real PG #514 text via Bash (47 chapters, "CHAPTER ONE…" + a doubled TOC) + reproducing the 1-page-skip live. **LIVE:** label increments only at true boundaries, spans many pages, matches content (ch12 = "Camp Laurence"); rapid-flip no longer skips; spot-checked Pride & Prejudice (61 ch, ch1=37pp, ch2=34pp, zero 1-page skips).
- **(2) REFLECT ICON — ADDED.** A Feather icon in the reader controls (inline + immersive) → opens an **anytime, context-aware** reflection via `ChapterEndCard` `overridePrompt`+`anytime`: "You're in Chapter N of <book>. What's staying with you…" (authored chapter prompt used when one exists; chapter-end guess/cohort hidden in anytime mode). LIVE-verified the prompt names the current chapter.
- **(3) CALMER SCROLL.** Swipe threshold 50→80px + require 1.6:1 horizontal dominance, plus the flip debounce — rapid flips coalesce, no twitchy double-advance.
- **(4) WATER SHEET −/+.** Clear Minus/Plus stepper buttons (Lucide) flanking the count, plus the "Add water" CTA. (Both were technically present before; rebuilt unambiguous.)
- **(5) MEAL SHEET = REAL LOGGER.** Method tabs **Search · Recents · Snap · Say · Scan** + a search field + a **Recent & quick-add** list (porridge/yogurt/salad/katsu/banana with kcal + add) + meal-type chips — not a bare line.
- **(6) CYCLE/SYMPTOM ELEVATED.** A prominent "Cycle & symptoms" block now sits right after the hero/Your-Day area (phase line + Log a symptom + Cycle calendar + Health-letters link); the buried lower health card was removed.
- **(7) CYCLE CALENDAR.** A calendar icon at the TOP opens a **beautiful impressionistic** month (`CycleCalendar`): soft phase-tinted rounded cells, past period filled + glow, **predicted** period dashed, today ink-ringed, phase legend, gentle "next period likely ~the 23rd" line. On-brand, not a clinical grid.
- **(8) STACK-SPREAD DECK.** Section cards are now a layered cascade (`StackSpread`) — each collapsed card ~20% beneath the previous with its accent edge; tap brings a card to front + expands (summary + actions + open link). LIVE-verified bring-to-front (tapped Horoscope → expanded).
- Files: `DailyStoryReader.jsx` (debounce + measure-guard + swipe threshold + Reflect button + goToChapter/onMarks already), `BookReader.jsx` (smartReflectPrompt + reflect wiring), `ChapterEndCard.jsx` (overridePrompt/anytime), `TodayDemo6.jsx` (water steppers, rich meal logger, cycle block, calendar icon + CycleCalendar, StackSpread). Build clean; 0 console errors; existing reader/demo behaviour intact. Screenshots → `femwell-handoff/{reader-reflect-anytime, today-top-cycle, today-cycle-and-stack, today-stack-deck, today-cycle-calendar, today-water-sheet, today-meal-sheet-rich}.png`.

## 🔁 BOOKREADER RETRY-ON-502 (cold-start self-heals) — SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-16) — live `index-CMDQm5Qx.js` · commit `7ccd299`
- **Fixes the flagged `fetchGutenbergBook` cold-start 502 (the reader was unusable until a manual reload).** Wrapped the invoke in a guarded retry loop: **up to 4 attempts, backoff 0.7s/1.4s/2.2s**, retrying only TRANSIENT failures (5xx/429/network/opaque cold-start; a genuine 4xx is not hammered; a structured `data.error` is treated as real, no retry). Calm escalating loading note ("the library's waking up — fetching the book…" → "still fetching — almost there…"). If it still fails: a gentle fail-soft "**Tap to try again**" (re-runs via `retryTick`).
- **LIVE-VERIFIED via fault-injected 502s (Playwright route):** (a) 2 transient 502s → **auto-recovers** to "Chapter 1 / 61" with the escalating note; (b) 4 persistent 502s → exhausts retries → calm "Tap to try again" state → **manual retry recovers**. Warm load unaffected (no regression: 514 still loads "Chapter 2 / 47"). Screenshot `femwell-handoff/reader-retry-failsoft.png`. File: `BookReader.jsx` only.

## 📖 BOOKS READER FIXES (1-4) + TODAY IN-PLACE LOG SHEETS (5) — SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-16) — live `index-cJKw1aRz.js` · commit `141f26b`
- **5 Halli-flagged fixes; read BRAND_IDENTITY gate + the reader/club/ReadingActivity code first. All live-verified at 390px.**
- **(1) CHAPTER ≠ PAGE — FIXED.** Root cause: `splitChapters` missed Little Women's spelled-out "CHAPTER ONE" headings → fell back to 500-word "pages", each treated as a chapter → the projective reflection (prompts exist for ch 0-5) fired every page turn. Fix: broadened detection (whole "CHAPTER …" line incl. spelled-out + standalone number words/Roman) + **collapse the table-of-contents** (keep last occurrence per heading label + ≥600-char min-body filter) so Little Women reads as **47 real chapters (was 94 = TOC+body doubled)**; and the reflection is **gated on real chapters only** (page-fallback never fires one). LIVE: "Chapter X / 47"; card appears ONLY crossing a true chapter boundary (1→2 fired the correct ch-2 prompt), never on within-chapter page flips (10 flips, 0 cards).
- **(2) TWO MARKS — ADDED.** A `MarksBar` shows the **smart daily-read schedule mark** ("Today's daily read · Chapter N" — ~1 ch/day from a device-local start, with behind/ahead status + a "Go to chapter N" jump) AND the **physical manual bookmark** (set via the ribbon — now also surfaced in the non-immersive controls, not just immersive). Reader gained `goToChapter` (external jump) + `onMarks` (reports live current/bookmarks) props. LIVE: bar shows "Today's daily read · Chapter 7 · 6 chapters behind — no rush" + "No bookmark yet — tap the ribbon".
- **(3) CATCH-UP — FIXED.** Reflections are **held** when bingeing (chapter reaches < 25s apart) OR behind the daily-read schedule — a calm "you're catching up — I'll hold the chapter reflections until you're current" note instead of a wall of prompts. LIVE: with start set 6 days back, crossing a boundary showed the catch-up note + **no card**.
- **(4) COMMUNITY DEEP-LINK — FIXED.** The in-club book's link now goes to the **actual Book Club** (`/Community?view=bookclub` — Community handles it), other books to their **own readers' corner** (`?club=dailyread-<id>`); the chapter-end card gained a **"Discuss this in the Book Club / readers' corner"** link. Never the generic Community page. LIVE: `?view=bookclub` lands on the Book Club (checkpoints/cadence); `?club=dailyread-11&title=Alice` lands on "Alice in Wonderland — readers' corner"; the in-card link renders.
- **(5) TODAY LOGS = IN-PLACE POP-UPS — FIXED (/TodayDemo6).** Log/write actions (meal, +water, leave a line, answer QOTD, post echo, log symptom, tonight's practice) now open an **in-place scroll-locked sheet ON Today** (`ActionSheet`, shared `useScrollLock`), write + confirm in place ("Logged — Meadowlight felt that") + **nourish the garden** (hero pill fires), **no navigation away**; a secondary "or open the full page" link remains. Read/open actions still navigate. LIVE: meal sheet opened (URL unchanged), saved → confirmation + garden pill (URL still unchanged); water/QOTD/line sheets all open in place.
- Files: `BookReader.jsx`, `DailyStoryReader.jsx` (additive props + non-immersive bookmark), `ChapterEndCard.jsx` (+discuss link, gold→#A8893F), `Community.jsx` (?view=bookclub), `TodayDemo6.jsx` (sheets). Build clean; existing reader functionality (immersive, settings, position save, headsup, crisis, prompts/cohort/reveal) intact. Screenshots → `femwell-handoff/{reader-reflection-card, reader-marks-behind, reader-catchup-note, demo6-meal-sheet}.png`.

The reader is unusable while it 502s. Worth a look (warm-up/timeout/retry on the function, or a client retry-on-502 in BookReader).

## ✅ READINGACTIVITY HARDENING SHIPPED (cap-free, via dispatcher actions) + GO-FORWARD PATTERN (2026-06-16) — commit `57f8ea4`, deploy `index-DC9jgEB2.js`
- **THE UNBLOCK (Halli's insight): the 50-fn cap only blocks NEW function NAMES — UPDATING an existing dispatcher with new `action` branches deploys fine.** So we do NOT need to reach ≤49. The pending ReadingActivity service-role hardening shipped by folding its write/read logic into the EXISTING `createCommunityPost` dispatcher as actions **`readingActivity.record` / `.cohort` / `.prediction`** (an update, not a new function) + locking the `ReadingActivity` RLS to **admin (read+create+update+delete)** + repointing the client (readingActivity.js). Sequenced safely: deploy dispatcher → site-deploy client → THEN entities-push the RLS lock (so the live client never broke). **LIVE-VERIFIED:** direct client write → **403 denied**; direct client read → 200 but **0 rows** (RLS hides all rows from non-admins); record + aggregate **via the dispatcher → 200** (asServiceRole); garden nourishment intact (device-local `fw_read_*`, untouched). Function count unchanged at **118** — cap never touched.
- **>>> GO-FORWARD PATTERN (canonical — under the 50-fn cap) <<<** ALL new backend logic rides an EXISTING function as a new dispatcher `action` (UPDATE, never a new function name). Pick a fitting existing dispatcher (e.g. `createCommunityPost` for community/content/books, `postEcho` for echo, `joinCircle` for circles), add `action: 'feature.verb'`, repoint the client `invoke()` to that fn+action, deploy (update). **The cap does NOT block future backend features — it only changes how they're added.** New ENTITIES are NOT capped (commit .jsonc + `base44 entities push`).
- **CONSOLIDATION: STOPPED — ≤49 is neither reachable (floor ~85–93; ~55–60 are essential cron/worker/orchestrator infra) NOR needed (go-forward pattern above).** The Echo/Circle/Community merges done (→118) are kept as good hygiene; NO further Tier-B/Tier-C/cron work. Payments cluster left untouched (off-limits per Halli). We are genuinely unblocked regardless of the Base44 plan answer.

## 🧮 FUNCTION CONSOLIDATION (132→≤49) — SUPERSEDED by the dispatcher-action pattern above; kept for history
**Why:** Base44 caps apps at 50 functions; this app is at 132, blocking ALL new-function deploys (incl. the pending ReadingActivity hardening `584a316`). Reaching ≤49 needs removing ~83 — a large, risky LIVE-backend refactor. Per Halli: correctness over speed, many batches OK, stop+report if a batch can't be safely verified.
- **INVENTORY (mapped):** 132 deployed · **60 client-invoked** (live feature flows — must repoint on merge) · **72 not client-invoked** (cron/workers/server-to-server/dead). **KEY RISK:** `pipelineOrchestrator` is a meta-dispatcher that calls many functions BY NAME (migrations, workers) — deleting/merging anything it references breaks a pipeline phase. Cron functions each carry their own schedule — merging them risks losing the trigger. So most of the 72 are NOT dead.
- **THE CAP-SAFE RECIPE (since new-fn deploys are blocked):** to merge a cluster, REPURPOSE one existing member's name as the `action`-routed dispatcher (a REDEPLOY of an existing name — allowed), repoint every client `invoke("member") → invoke("dispatcher",{action})`, deploy dispatcher + site-deploy client, probe+verify the flow, THEN delete the other members (frees slots). Net −(members−1)/cluster. Pure-delete for confirmed-dead (no dispatcher).
- **RISK TIERS / PLAN:**
  - **Tier A — pure delete of confirmed-dead (safest, do first):** one-off migrations (DONE: 3 deleted) + verify-then-delete completed backfills (`backfill*` ×6) + seeds (`seedPlannerTestAccount`, `seedPodcasts`) + `purgeTestPosts`. ~10 slots. (migratePlannerPhase2/migrateSessionsToPractice are referenced by pipelineOrchestrator → leave or de-list there first.)
  - **Tier B — client feature dispatchers (medium, verify via the flow):** witness(7→1), twin(5→1), club(4→1), echo(4→1), community addComment/createCommunityPost/reactCommunity/reportCommunity(4→1), circle(2→1), game openGameRoundV2/submitGameResponse/finalizeGameRound(3→1), voice generateVoiceScript/postVoiceNote/voiceTTS/generateAudio(4→1), astro askStars/generateHoroscopeReading/generateCompatibility(3→1), jess jessSupport/generateAssistantSuggestion/generateJessHero/guideActions/guideVoiceSession(5→1), nutrition analyzeMeal/analyzeMealPhoto/generateMealPlan(3→1), payments stripeCheckout/createOneShotCheckout(+stripeWebhook own endpoint)(→1). ~40 slots.
  - **Tier C — cron/worker clusters (RISKY, do last, may leave some):** lifestyle ingest/rank/summarize (~15), insights/correlation (~10), notifications/cron senders (~8), fiction (~6). Each has cron schedules + inter-fn + orchestrator by-name refs → highest break risk; consolidate only with per-fn trigger analysis.
  - **Estimate:** Tier A+B ≈ 129→~80; reaching 49 needs Tier C too. **Multi-turn track.**
- **CAP CONCLUSIVELY RE-CONFIRMED (2026-06-16):** deployed a trivial throwaway `capProbeTest` (1 line) at 129 deployed → **`Error deploying function "capProbeTest": Maximum of 50 functions per app reached`**. So the cap is REAL + a DEPLOY-TIME block only (the app runs fine at 125+ at runtime — consistent with Halli's note). It's stated as a hard "50 per app". **Whether a PLAN UPGRADE raises it is NOT determinable from the CLI/API** (no plan/limits command; app-meta has no function-limit/tier field). >>> **RECOMMEND HALLI ASK BASE44 whether a higher plan raises the 50-fn cap BEFORE the risky Tier-B merges — if it does, upgrading moots the entire ~40-function consolidation (far cheaper + safer).** <<<
- **BATCH 1 (commit `b63954e`):** deleted 3 dead migrations → 132→129. Zero collateral.
- **BATCH 2 — Tier A (commit `214cd9f`):** deleted 4 more confirmed-dead (backfillImages, backfillMealNutrition, seedPlannerTestAccount, purgeTestPosts — all 0 refs, not in pipelineOrchestrator) → **129 → 125**. Tier re-probed: postEcho 400, openGameRoundV2 200, pipelineOrchestrator 403, deleted ones 404 — **zero collateral**. (Remaining Tier-A candidates backfillLongreads/Podcast/TikTok/YouTube + seedPodcasts each have 1 ref = pipelineOrchestrator by-name → would need de-listing there first; left.)
- **BATCH 3 — Tier B, first dispatcher merge (commit `58ac24f`, deploy `index-BG-837PT.js`):** consolidated the **Echo cluster** — `postEcho` repurposed as an `action`-dispatcher (post|react|report|retract), merged faithfully from reactEcho/reportEcho/retractEcho (same asServiceRole anonymity, crisis re-check, rate-limit, auto-hide, author_hash ownership); EchoWall.jsx + ShareAsEchoSheet.jsx repointed to postEcho+action. Then deleted reactEcho/reportEcho/retractEcho → **125 → 122**. Tier re-probed: postEcho 400, deleted 3 → 404, answerQotd 400, openGameRoundV2 200, pipelineOrchestrator 403 — **zero collateral**. Tier re-probed: joinCircle 400, leaveCircle 404, postEcho 400, answerQotd 400, pipelineOrchestrator 403 — zero collateral.

- **⚠️ CRITICAL MATH — ≤49 is likely NOT reachable by consolidation alone without breaking live infra.** Of ~118: merging ALL remaining client clusters (twin −4, witness −6, club −3, jess −4, nutrition −2, voice −3, astro −2, payments −1 ≈ −25) lands ~**93**. The other ~55–60 are **cron jobs / workers / server-to-server / pipelineOrchestrator-referenced infra** (ingest*, rank*, summarize*, refresh*, send*Reminders, generate* insights, *Worker, compute*, period/cleanup/reap/resolve, etc.) — each runs a scheduled/background feature and is NOT safely removable. - **The fix IS correct + in the repo:** share card `background: T.cream` (undefined → transparent) → `background: T.paperHi` (#F4EFE3 solid cream); export bg rect → `T.paper`. Verified present in `3755dad`/`78997d7`. Just can't reach live until the build unwedges.

## 🌿 COMPANION FIXES — clean share + meaningful garden logic (2026-06-15)
**Halli flagged: (1) Share rendered broken (bleed-through, overlap, clipped text); (2) the garden's logic was hollow — a manual "Tend" that just flips a flag is meaningless.**
- **(1) SHARE — fixed + upgraded to real image export.** Rewrote the share modal: full-cover, **opaque scrim (rgba 0.9, zIndex 1000) → no bleed-through**, body **scroll-locked** while open, opening Share closes the customise/ritual panels, layout legible, nothing clipped (gentle line uses `<br/>`, footer full). **Phase-3 "share-card image export" delivered now:** "Save image" builds a self-contained SVG card (cream bg + the live bloom markup + non-personal text) → rasterises to **PNG** via canvas → Web Share (where `navigator.canShare` files) else download. Non-personal artifact only (name + bloom + gentle line — no email/cycle/journal/mood). `exportImage()` in NurtureGarden; sets `window.__fwSharePNGBytes` for verification.
- **(2) GARDEN LOGIC — redesigned to be genuinely connected. THE LOGIC (Halli's "what's the logic?"):**
  - **What nourishes it:** real acts of living in the app — a journal line, a logged meal/water, a daily check-in, a cycle note, answering the community question, planning your day, keeping a practice. Read across the app (guarded, private). The companion is the visible sum of your real self-care; nothing fake, nothing done *for the plant*.
  - **Stages = showing up over time:** seed→sprout→budding→blooming→full bloom, driven by cumulative real engagement; never a score, never a streak, never decreases.
  - **Cause→effect made VISIBLE:** a new "What's feeding your garden" panel says what nourished it — *"Today, your journal and your check-in fed it"* + a week summary + "N days you showed up". Compact home cards show a one-line "Today, … fed it".
  - **Tend, reimagined:** the meaningless flag-tap is GONE. Growth comes from genuinely living in the app (the acts ARE the tending). The one optional ritual is **"Leave a line"** — naming how you feel in one honest line; it's saved cross-device (CompanionState.tended_note) and counts as showing up today. Real act, not a tap.
  - **No-guilt ethic intact:** never dies; 5+ day gap = resting season + warm welcome-back; growth never decreases.
  - `CompanionState` gained a `tended_note` field (re-synced). Files: `companion.js`, `NurtureGarden.jsx`, `CompanionState.jsonc`.
- **SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED — final bundle `index-DTZOd_GY.js`** (commits `5cf15ba` redesign, `8be0587` export-fix). Export-fix: the `<img>` SVG loader uses a STRICT XML parser, so outerHTML+regex (missing xmlns + duplicate width) → `img.onerror`, no PNG; fixed by cloning the bloom node, setting xmlns/width/height, and `XMLSerializer`. **VERIFIED live:** (a) Share modal clean — opaque scrim, no bleed-through, nothing clipped — and **"Save image" produced a 412KB PNG** (`window.__fwSharePNGBytes`); (b) garden shows **"Today, the line you left and the day you planned fed it"** + week summary; **"Leave a line" ritual end-to-end** (typed a line → reflected back + persisted to `CompanionState.tended_note`, survived reload); hollow "Tend/Tended today" gone. Screenshots in femwell-handoff: `companion-fix-garden-logic_`, `companion-fix-share-card_`, `companion-fix-share-card-live_20260615.png`. (Test user = Meadowlight/peony with a left line; harmless.)

## 🚀 RELIABLE ONE-COMMAND DEPLOY — `node scripts/deploy.mjs` (no Chrome / no builder) (2026-06-15)
- **The flaky builder "Publish" button and the Chrome-extension dependency are no longer needed.** `scripts/deploy.mjs`: reads the platform OAuth token from `~/.base44/auth/auth.json` → auto-refreshes it via `oauth/token` (grant_type=refresh_token) when expired → `POST /api/apps/<id>/deploy` (the same call Publish makes) → polls femwells.com for the bundle flip.
- **Deploy auth = a PLATFORM OAuth token, NOT the api_key.** Confirmed by reading the published `base44` CLI (npm `base44@0.0.55`): it auths only via `BASE44_ACCESS_TOKEN`/`BASE44_REFRESH_TOKEN` or device-code login (client `base44_cli`, scope apps:read/write); BASE44_API_KEY 401s on /deploy (it's only the SDK data API).
- **Setup (once, secure, nothing pasted in chat):** `npm i -g base44 && base44 login` → confirm the device code at app.base44.com/login/device → writes `~/.base44/auth/auth.json` (HOME dir, outside the repo, uncommittable; `.gitignore` already covers `.env*`). Token lasts ~30 days, auto-refreshes (refresh token rotates + is saved back); if it ever fully lapses, re-run `base44 login`.
- **TESTED end-to-end 2026-06-15:** logged in fresh (token aud=platform, exp 2026-07-15) by driving the connected Chrome through the device-code page; `node scripts/deploy.mjs` → state `ready` → deploy POST **200**. (Bundle stayed `index-BUpa-SqZ.js` because the code was identical to the live deploy — a no-op redeploy keeps the same hash, which the script now reports as success, not a timeout.)
- Memory: `base44-deploy-auth-mechanism.md`.

## 🎲 W1.a COMMUNITY GAMES BUILT OUT — 6 named, playable games (LIVE + verified) (2026-06-14) — live `index-D8NjKBim.js` · commits `3120e37 e96bfeb 49e4ba7 898174d 0705167 83b3692 804d48e`
- **WAS:** The Games Room = one nightly round + a dashed "More games — coming" placeholder (the worst-offender stub from the audit). **NOW:** a full lineup of named games — **This or That, One Word, Caption the Feeling, One-Line Story, Tiny Confession, Comfort Pick** — each Jess-hosted, playable end-to-end, with a warm count-free reveal, no leaderboard. Placeholder removed. LIVE-VERIFIED at 390px: all 6 show "playing now" (This or That = 2 option buttons; other 5 = one-line input + "Add mine"); played This or That → "Cosy night in" → options cleared + reveal "your answer's in" shown.
- **BACKEND SAGA (read before touching games):** extended `openGameRound` with a `{kind}` param (per-game `lighter:<kind>` namespace). Opening 6 fresh rounds fired 6 concurrent `InvokeLLM` calls → the function HUNG (the `base44-fetch-no-timeout-hangs` trap: awaited LLM wedges the socket even with a timeout race, and a wedged shared instance blocks siblings → all 7 calls hung/503). Removing the LLM didn't recover it (same-name redeploys couldn't clear the wedged registration) → **renamed to `openGameRoundV2`** (LLM-free: curated static prompts + static reveal). V2 ALSO hung (function tier saturated by the earlier hung-request storm). **Resolution: local-first client design** — `GameRoundCard` SEEDS a playable local round in `useState` (module-level `makeLocalRound`), so games are playable from first render regardless of backend health; `openGameRoundV2` upgrades to the real cross-device round in the background when the tier recovers. (A useEffect-seeded version left `round` null in the embedded-sheet context — the useState seed was the fix.)
- **CURRENT STATE:** games are fully playable CLIENT-LOCAL (device answers + warm reveal). Cross-device aggregate activates automatically once the base44 function tier (`openGameRoundV2`) un-wedges — it was still hanging at ship time (likely needs the hung-request backlog to drain or base44 support). Old `openGameRound` folder left in place (unused, wedged).
- Build+eslint green throughout. Deploy chain ended **`index-D8NjKBim.js`**. Locked constraints honoured (no leaderboard, anonymity, no emoji, cream/plum).

## 🐛 2 JOURNAL-HUB BUGS FIXED + AUDITED APP-WIDE — on-dark text contrast · "background under the background" scroll (2026-06-14) — live `index-BvQCIrn3.js` · commits `431a1b2` `98f9aa8`
- **BUG 1 — dark text on the dusk Jess-prompt card (unreadable).** Root cause: JournalHub places the *light-surface* `PromptCarousel` on a `T.dusk` card; PromptCarousel's inline dark-ink tokens (`T.ink`/`T.muted`) overrode the card's inherited light colour. **FIX:** added a reusable `onDark` prop to `PromptCarousel` → on a dark surface it uses `T.paper`(cream)/`T.wax` for prompt, eyebrow, CTAs, footer (and `carve` relief off); default-light path unchanged. JournalHub passes `onDark`. **LIVE-VERIFIED (390px DOM):** dusk card `rgb(33,26,18)` now renders text `rgb(236,231,218)`(paper) + `rgb(239,227,201)`(wax) — was dark ink `rgb(11,8,5)`. Contrast ~8:1.
- **BUG 2 — "background under the background" scrolling separately (parallax artifact).** Root cause: two paper-texture sources used `background-attachment:fixed` → texture pinned to viewport while content scrolled, doubled where a page also set an inline paper bg. **FIX (app-wide, source-level):** (a) Editorial `PAPER_BG` constant `fixed → scroll`; (b) the global `#main-content > div` rule in `index.css` `fixed → scroll` (this was the live culprit on pages w/o an inline override, e.g. Lifestyle); (c) JournalDemo1 inline copy for consistency. Now one coherent bg that scrolls with the page. **LIVE-VERIFIED (390px DOM, new bundle):** `background-attachment:fixed` element count = **0** on Journal, Nutrition, Community, AND Lifestyle (Lifestyle was the lone `fixed` before the index.css fix).
- **APP-WIDE AUDIT (Halli: "not unique to Journal"):** swept Journal/Nutrition/Community/Lifestyle for both bugs. No other genuine dark-on-dark text found — the one Community hit ("Answer today's question") is a **false positive**: it's the gold CTA button (`bg rgb(168,137,63)` gold + `rgb(36,26,38)` plum) — intentional brand CTA, legible, not a regression (noted; borderline-AA on small text, left for an Atelier/brand call, not in scope).
- Screenshots: live before = `journal-bugs-before.png` (prior turn). The SPA's persistent connection blocks screenshot capture (`document_idle` never settles), so the after-state is proven by exact-rgb DOM measurement on the live `index-BvQCIrn3.js` bundle (stronger evidence than a pixel grab for a contrast claim).
- Build+eslint green. Deploy chain: bug1 `431a1b2` → `index-CrbVi7wJ.js`; bug2/app-wide `98f9aa8` → **`index-BvQCIrn3.js`** (deploy 200, hash flipped, verified). Locked constraints honoured.

## 🔒 SCROLL-BLEED FIXED APP-WIDE — every overlay inherits the shared scroll-lock (2026-06-14) — live `index-CsSJoH5q.js` · commits `137c6be` `58f83e0` `30d1988`
- **THE BUG:** scrolling a sheet's content scrolled the BACKGROUND page instead (scroll-bleed), leaving the overlay's words behind. Classic missing body-scroll-lock.
- **THE FIX (once, shared):** new `src/utils/useScrollLock.js` — ref-counted, position:fixed body lock (iOS-safe; restores exact scroll position; nested/multiple overlays unlock only on the last close). Each overlay's scroll area also gets `overflow-y:auto` + bounded `maxHeight` + **`overscroll-behavior:contain`** + `-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch`, so the sheet is the scroller and never bleeds.
- **COVERAGE — ALL ~27 overlays app-wide:** core (commit 137c6be) = HubSheet (every hub bottom-sheet + logger + in-sheet rooms), the 3 jump-to switchers (Nutrition/Journal/Community), 18+ AgeGate. Long-tail (58f83e0 + 30d1988, subagent) = CheckinModal, MenuSheet, AssistantOverlay, JessVoiceMode, JessSettingsSheet, JessVoiceLogger, MorningBriefOverlay, ExpandedPlayer, PodcastListenSheet, DailyStoryReader, Lifestyle ArticleSheet, SmartLoggerV4, UnifiedTabLogger, NewEntrySheet, EntryReader, FourLivesChooser, ShareAsEchoSheet, SealedLetterCompose, BreakSealReader, PhaseTwin, AskForWitnessSheet, WitnessInbox, Community CrisisSheet/ShareToSheet, horoscope BirthDataSheet (×2). Ad-hoc `document.body.style.overflow` locks were replaced with the consistent ref-counted hook. (RoomComposer left — it's an inline card, not a fixed overlay.)
- **LIVE-VERIFIED (390px mobile touch):** HubSheet — page@180 → body fixed@-180, sheet content scrolled to 300, forced window.scrollTo(600) left bg at 0, close restored to 180. CheckinModal — body fixed@-150, forced scroll stayed 0. MenuSheet — body fixed@-160, forced scroll stayed 0. SCREENSHOTS `scroll-lock-sheet-open_20260614.png` · `scroll-lock-menusheet_20260614.png`.
- Build+eslint green (cleared pre-existing unused imports in the touched files: SmartLoggerV4/MenuSheet/DailyStoryReader). Deploy: `index-DohPdo_6.js` → `137c6be` → `58f83e0` → **`index-CsSJoH5q.js`** (30d1988).

## 🐞 LOGGING-MOVES-TOTALS BUG FIX + 👆 DIRECT-ON-CARD ACTIONS + ↕ CARD SCROLL — SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (2026-06-14) — live `index-1Usdu1Sa.js` · commit `a861d20` (+ demo polish `95d2883`)

## 📋 NUTRITION PAGE — WHAT'S LEFT (precise, ~88% complete) — as of 2026-06-14
Honest remaining-work list for Halli to relay. The nutrition page itself (the rebuild) is ~88%; the *cross-app* ties beyond nutrition are thinner.
- **Within-page polish (~12%):**
  1. **Richer photo-to-recipe** — photo logging works (gpt-4o-mini vision → editable draft), but "snap → here's a recipe to cook from it" isn't built.
  2. **More CoFID foods** — the floor covers ~85 common UK foods; less-common/branded/composite dishes (e.g. "katsu curry", "pad thai") won't match → no micro estimate for those. Expand the table over time.
  3. **Deeper cycle memory** — cycle-aware nudges are qualitative + current-phase only; no across-cycle learning ("you tend to crave X in your luteal phase") yet.
  4. **Empty-state polish** — a brand-new user with zero logs sees several honest "nothing yet" cards; could be warmer/more guided.
- **Cross-APP connectivity beyond nutrition (~20% → still the thinnest seam):**
  5. **Nutrition ↔ Journal** — Progress correlates food×mood/energy from DailyCheckins (one-directional read). No "log how this meal made you feel" bridge INTO the journal, and the journal doesn't surface nutrition context.
  6. **Nutrition ↔ Planner/Today** — the hub day-stepper is local; not yet wired to a single app-wide "Today/Planner" so a meal logged in Nutrition shows on the main Today timeline.
  7. **Nutrition ↔ Doctor-Export** — the GP/doctor-ready export doesn't yet include a nutrition summary (energy/iron/protein trends, the women's-layer leans) for an appointment.
- **Known rough edges (all currently acceptable / mitigated):**
  8. **Generation latency** — a 7-day plan takes ~35–45s on gpt-4o-mini; mitigated with slimmed output + a 60s guard + an honest "up to a minute" note, but it's still a slow moment (could move to a faster model or background job).
  9. **Plan-cell "Log today" / "you loved this"** only render on a populated plan / for genuinely-loved meals (correct, but invisible until there's data).
  10. **CoFID estimates are generic portions**, not the user's exact plate — labelled as estimates, but precision is inherently limited without exact logging.

## 🧪 STREAM A SEAM 5 — UK CoFID LAB-GRADE MICRONUTRIENT FLOOR — SHIPPED + SCREENSHOT-PROVEN (2026-06-14) — live `index-DA-esXxg.js` · commit `e0cd33c`
- **The women's layer is now data-driven even for plainly-typed meals.** New `src/utils/cofid.js` = a curated UK food-composition table (~85 common foods — greens/pulses/meat/fish/dairy/grains/nuts — per-100g iron·folate·calcium·fibre + macros, from McCance & Widdowson / CoFID, Crown copyright, public domain) + a length-priority matcher: `cofidFloorMicros("lentil & spinach soup, wholemeal roll")` → resolves spinach + lentils + bread at typical UK portions (iron 9.6mg, folate 323µg…). Pure, no network — the table IS the local cache.
- **Wired as a FLOOR into the spine (read-time, retroactive, no writes):** `dayNutrition`/`rangeNutrition` take `{floor:true}`; `mealMicros` backfills micros (+ fibre) NOT backed by a real logged value from CoFID, flagged `estimated` (never counted as `known`). `UnifiedInsightsTab` opts in (`rangeNutrition(week,7,{floor:true})`) + shows an honest **"Some figures are estimated from typical UK food-composition values (CoFID)…"** note when the floor contributed. **The hub header stays real-only (no floor)** so the headline iron count is never overstated.
- **LIVE-PROVEN @390px** `connectivity-seam5-cofid-floor_20260614.png`: the women's layer reads the test user's logged meals through the floor and flags **Fibre "LIGHTER THIS WEEK"** (data-driven, was hardcoded copy) with the CoFID estimate note. Node-verified matcher (multi-food, "sweet potato">"potato", no false positives).
- Guarded, real data, honesty-labelled, no scoreboards/emoji. Build+eslint green (0 errors). Deploy: `index-YyzgCjuo.js` → **`index-DA-esXxg.js`**.
- **Stream A is now substantially complete** (spine, women's micros, derived targets+height, personalisation, all 5 connectivity seams + CoFID floor). The nutrition page's data genuinely compounds end-to-end.

## 🔗 STREAM A CONNECTIVITY — 4 SEAMS (logged→planner, shop/pantry→generator, recipe→plan, mood/cycle→insights) — SHIPPED + SCREENSHOT-PROVEN (2026-06-14) — live `index-YyzgCjuo.js` · commits `b15f5b0`/`e5a2f53`/`aa71cc3`/`428beb5`/`b43aa18`/`48e8b0d`
- **SEAM 1 — logged→planner feedback (loved meals resurface):** `personalisation.lovedMeals` ranks meals by recency-weighted frequency × meal_score (rated ≥6.5 OR returned-to 2+ days = loved; internal ranking only, no scoreboard). Generator now receives `loved_meals`; the plan editor leads chips with a heart; a filled cell the user has loved shows a "you loved this" badge. **ALSO fixed a real latent bug:** `usual_meals` was sent as a per-slot object but the fn called `.join` on it → threw → generation 500'd for any user with history (`flattenMeals` now accepts array OR object). SCREENSHOT `connectivity-seam1-loved-meal-resurfaced_20260614.png` — generated plan with **"Mushroom risotto" resurfaced + ♥ "you loved this"** badge + the per-cell "Log today".
- **SEAM 2 — shop/pantry→generator:** pantry (ShoppingList source:pantry) feeds the generator as `ingredients` (builds on what's in → shorter list); optional weekly **£ budget** in the generator, persisted durably to NutritionProfile (`budget_weekly`), passed to the fn → prompt keeps the week within budget on affordable UK staples. SCREENSHOT `connectivity-seam2-pantry-budget_20260614.png`.
- **SEAM 3 — recipe→"add to this week's plan":** RecipeObject gains a day+meal picker that writes the recipe name+macros into `MealPlans.plan_days[day][slot]` + `cell_macros` (the legacy shape the planner reads) — so a discovered/saved recipe flows into the planner → shop → one-tap log. New cycle-aware section: `cyclePhaseNudge` surfaces a gentle phase-appropriate FOOD lean (menstrual/follicular/ovulatory/luteal) only when the phase is genuinely known + the stage cycles — qualitative leans only, NO macro prescriptions, "a gentle lean, not a rule", not medical advice. SCREENSHOT `connectivity-seam4-cycle-nudge_20260614.png` (Menstrual lean; captured by briefly seeding the test profile's `last_period_start_date` then **restoring it to null** — test account left exactly as found).
- **GENERATION PERF FIX (made the whole loop usable):** the 7-day generator was timing out — fn returned 200 but the client guard (20s, then 35s) discarded valid plans (gpt-4o-mini took ~35–60s with the heavy `cook_steps` output). Fixed by (a) **slimming meal_plan output to names+macros** (drop cook_steps/descriptions the grid never uses → ~70% less output, far faster; recipe mode keeps full detail), and (b) raising the client guard to **60s** + an honest "up to a minute" note. Generation now fills the grid end-to-end (proven live).

Build+eslint green (0 errors). Deploy chain: `index-Do58vUcd.js` → `…DQT5J7Ho` → `…CYQl9sQ_` → **`index-YyzgCjuo.js`**.

## 🎛️ CONTROL-CENTER DEMOS REDONE TO SPEC (vertical, one-card-per-feature) — SHIPPED + SCREENSHOT-PROVEN (2026-06-14) — live `index-CPMnLKpE.js` · commits `23c9d12` + `ce2cafb`
- **WHY:** Halli: the old Control-Center demos were "not how it is." The old build squeezed ALL features into a 2-col HORIZONTAL grid inside one floating card — wrong. Apple's Control Center is a VERTICAL stack of large modules, ONE per screen, swiped up/down.
- **CORRECTED CONCEPT (built):** new shared `src/pages/controlCenterKit.jsx` = a HEADER summary page at top, then a VERTICAL scroll-snap pager of FULL-COVER rounded cards — ONE FEATURE PER CARD, each large enough to hold that single feature richly inline; swipe UP → next feature full-screen. A right-pinned vertical icon DOT rail = position + jump. Grip handle + "swipe up · N cards, one each" cue + per-card "↓ next" label + a peek of the next card. FemWell editorial (cream/plum paper, Ephesis/Cormorant, Lucide, soft lift) — NO Apple dark glass, NO emoji, NO horizontal grid, NO scoreboards.

Each card body holds the real inline surface (Log = 6 method tiles + recents + red action; Today = energy ring + macro bars + logged meals; Witness = dusk holding card; QOTD = question + anon answers; rooms = ambient + sample posts + enter), not a thin summary.
- Self-contained demos (useState/useRef/useEffect only, mock data, no base44/network). Build+eslint green.

## 🎯 REAL TDEE TARGETS (Stream A #3) + 🧠 PERSONALISATION/MEMORY (Stream A #4) — SHIPPED + SCREENSHOT-PROVEN (2026-06-14) — live `index-DCCgIEVZ.js` · commits `48f7102` (#3) + `5ed25fd` (#4)
- **STREAM A #3 — REAL DERIVED TARGETS (replaces the flat /2000 fallback):** new `src/utils/nutritionTargets.js` → `deriveTargets({profile, nutritionProfile, bodyMetrics})` returns `{energy_kcal, energy_min, energy_max, protein_g, fibre_g, iron_mg, calcium_mg, folate_ug, derived, basis, userSet, guideLine}`. Energy via **Mifflin-St Jeor** (female 10·kg+6.25·cm−5·age−161 × activity × gentle goal nudge, floored ~1500) expressed as a **RANGE**; **stage tilts** (protein 1.2g/kg peri/meno/postpartum/pregnant; iron 14.8mg menstruating vs 8.7 post-meno; fibre 30g; calcium 700–1000mg; folate 400µg pre-conception); user-set NutritionProfile values **override per-field**; gentle "a guide, never a cap" framing — no scoreboard. Wired into NutritionHub (loads latest BodyMetrics → header ring + macro targets + basis line), UnifiedMealPlanTab macro preview, UnifiedInsightsTab micro refs. **LIVE-VERIFIED @390px:** header macro row now reads **/70g protein · /30g fibre · /15mg iron** (stage-derived, was flat) + My Plan card **"1840–2160 kcal · gentle energy guide"** + basis **"Reproductive years — tuned to your stage for the week, never a cap."** (Note: height not in schema → most users get the stage-based default band, derived:false.)
- **STREAM A #4 — PERSONALISATION / MEMORY:** new `src/utils/personalisation.js` (recency-weighted: weight=1+0.6·e^(−ageDays/10) so frequency leads, recency tilts ties; counts are internal ranking only — NO scoreboard) → `topFoods`, `frequentFavourites`, `slotSuggestions`, `mealTimePattern`, `rankRecipesByPreference`, `learnedPatternLine`; reuses `foodModel.normalizeFood` for keys. Wired: UnifiedLogger ranks Recents by topFoods + surfaces frequentFavourites; UnifiedMealPlanTab editor uses slotSuggestions + feeds usual_meals to the generator; UnifiedRecipesTab ranks via rankRecipesByPreference; UnifiedInsightsTab adds a learnedPatternLine. **LIVE-VERIFIED @390px:** Recents reordered by preference (Mushroom risotto leads) + Progress card **"6 go-to meals you return to"** (frequentFavourites surfaced, framed "a quiet strength, not a rut").
- **Guardrails honoured:** real data only (every read .catch-guarded, every write withTimeout-guarded), no mock numbers, no streaks/scores/vanity counts, no emoji, cream/plum, Ephesis/Cormorant, Lucide/SVG, women's-layer honesty (no cycle-syncing macro prescriptions). Per-feature rebuilds untouched.

## 🔧 analyzeMeal STALL — root-caused + hardened + DEPLOYED (2026-06-13) · commit `824ee9a` · deployed 15:48:48
- **Root cause (code review):** `base44/functions/analyzeMeal/entry.ts` had **unguarded awaits at the top** — `base44.auth.me()` and `req.json()` — so a stalled platform call wedged the whole function with NO response (never reaching its internal LLM guard) = the >60s hang. Plus a **timeout mismatch** (client 18s < function LLM 20s → a slow-but-working analysis was abandoned client-side) and **500-on-failure** (no clean degrade).
- **Fix (deployed):** guard `auth.me()` (5s) + `req.json()` (4s) → fast 503/400 instead of hang; lower OpenAI guard **20s→13s** (below client's 18s) on both short+full paths; on any LLM slowness/error **return a clean 200 `{analysis_unavailable:true}`** instead of 500; client (NutritionTodayTab) shows a tidy "Meal saved — couldn't estimate calories just now" note and keeps the meal. `deno lint` clean (only pre-existing style warns).
- **NOT the gated dependency:** `OPENAI_API_KEY` is shared by **18 functions** (generateMealPlan, screenContent, generateInsights…) that have worked → the key is set/valid; analyzeMeal isn't uniquely key-blocked.
- **⚠️ Live-verify BLOCKED by 2 tooling walls (not evidence the fix failed):** (1) injected `fetch` to the `femwells.com/api` proxy hangs regardless (only the SPA's own SDK path works — can't measure the function directly from the browser); (2) the heavy NutritionHub SPA tab's **event loop wedges under CDP automation** — the meal SAVE resolves in an early window (3 test meals persisted) but the async analyze→MealLog.update can't complete/observe on the automated tab, so all 3 test meals show 0 kcal. A real (non-automated) device wouldn't have this freeze. **Could NOT positively confirm a real kcal renders.** The fix is correct by construction (never hangs; calories or clean state ≤~14s) and live.
- **NEEDS HALLI (quick, on your phone):** log a meal on femwells.com/NutritionHub → confirm within a few seconds it shows EITHER a real kcal OR the clean "couldn't estimate calories just now" note (no endless spinner). If it consistently says "couldn't estimate," that's OpenAI analysis latency (tune the 13s up, or check OpenAI quota) — but it will no longer hang. Test data in ojihalliburton57: 3 meals + 250ml hydration, today (clear anytime).

## ✅✅ POSTING SAGA — CLOSED (2026-06-12) — confirmed fixed end-to-end live · cleanups in `a29ad35`
**Posting works + persists** (UI compose → appears instantly → persists on reload; direct create 1.5s/200 + id). The bug was THREE stacked root causes, each masking the next — only fixed once all three were:
1. **RLS write-lock denied ALL service writes app-wide** — the B3 audit set `user_condition:{role:"system"}` on 22 entities; `asServiceRole` is admin-level, not "system", so every service create/update/delete was denied. → **`role:"admin"`** (`8d4f79f`). *Found by createPostDiag's "Permission denied" + Echo control.*
2. **Unguarded, unindexed `-created_date` rate-cap read hung the function** — even after RLS, an awaited sorted read with no index never settled (a `.catch` only catches a throw). → **timeout-guard every entity read/create** (`d1eeafc`) + **`created_date` index** on 10 community entities (`661e338`).
3. **Client gated the success UI on the slow refetch** — composer sat in "Posting…" ~16s, post showed only on reload. → **optimistic insert + background (non-awaited) refetch** for posts/comments/circle/club/club-notes; pill/close-week/QOTD already instant (`661e338`).
- **Cleanups (`a29ad35`):** removed the temporary `createPostDiag` probe; added **`purgeTestPosts`** (ADMIN-ONLY, hides leftover QA rows by id or body-prefix — reversible, since client owner-deletes are now correctly blocked by the role:admin lock).
- **Process lesson (logged to memory [[base44-service-role-rls-admin]]):** the exit-gate MUST include a real end-to-end WRITE that you read back — reads-200 + function-registration-400 hid all three bugs for ages.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** deploy the latest (functions: remove createPostDiag, add purgeTestPosts; entities already synced for the index). Optional: call `purgeTestPosts` (admin) to hide the QA rows ("qa …"/"[diag]"), or dashboard-purge. Then the saga is fully closed.

### (history below — the chain that got us here)
## ✅ POSTING WORKS + PERSISTS (2026-06-12) — UX polish: instant-show + fast reads — `661e338`
**Posting confirmed live:** direct createCommunityPost → 1.5s/200 + id; a direct test post AND a real UI post both appear in the Lounge after reload + persist. RLS fix + read-timeout-guard fixed the SAVE. Remaining was pure UX: composer sat in "Posting…" ~16s and the post only showed on reload, because the success UI was gated behind the slow unindexed `-created_date` feed refetch.
- **FIX (`661e338`), two parts:**
  - **CLIENT — optimistic + non-blocking:** on the create's 200, close composer + insert the returned post/comment into the feed IMMEDIATELY; background refetch is never awaited and no longer tick-remounts (which had forced a stale-feed flash + blocking read). Room feed (`onPostCreated` prepend), Circle + Club feeds (prepend), comments (append returned comment; was `await loadComments()` — the gate), club notes (append; was awaited). Verified already-instant: kindness pill (optimistic + rollback), close-week (`setClosed` instant), QOTD (`setAnswered` instant).
  - **ROOT — fast sorted reads:** added a `created_date` index to all 10 community entities whose feed/rate/dedup queries sort by `-created_date`/`created_date` (the unindexed sort was the slow/hanging read behind both the server rate-cap and the client refetches): CommunityPost, Comment, ClubNote, QotdResponse, RitualContribution, GameResponse, Club, GoalContribution, BookClubPick, WisdomEntry. All 10 JSONC validated.
  - eslint 0 errors, vite build green.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** deploy the BUNDLE (client) **and SYNC the 10 ENTITIES** (index change). Then drive a real UI post + comment: require composer closes + post/comment appears in **<2s, no reload**, and persists on reload. (Once the index is confirmed making reads fast, we can later relax the 2.5s read-timeout guards in the functions — they become belt-and-braces.)

## 🩹 RATE-CAP READ WAS THE LEFTOVER HANG — ALL ENTITY READS NOW GUARDED (2026-06-12) — `d1eeafc` · the timeout-guards stay (belt-and-braces); index now makes the reads themselves fast
RLS fix confirmed live ✅ — createPostDiag now returns ALL steps + Echo control `ok:true` (~200-350ms). But createCommunityPost THE FUNCTION still hung 27s+. Read the deployed file line-by-line: refactor IS in place (no OpenAI fetch, no awaited screenContent/jessSupport). The one awaited call createPostDiag does NOT exercise = the **rate-cap READ** `CommunityPost.filter({author_hash}, '-created_date', 50)` — **unguarded**. createPostDiag does raw create+delete only, never this filter → diag fast, function wedged. (`.catch` only catches a THROW; a hung read never settles. The create was already withTimeout'd, so the hang was BEFORE it.)
- **FIX (`d1eeafc`):** timeout-guard EVERY awaited platform read AND create on ALL write paths (fail-open on a read timeout so a hung anti-flood/dedup read can never block a write):
  - createCommunityPost: rate-cap read (2.5s) + club-validate read (2.5s); create already guarded.
  - addComment: post-lookup `.get` was unguarded AND the create was NOT guarded → both now guarded.
  - closeTheWeek: once-per-week dedup read (same `-created_date` shape) + create.
  - submitGameResponse: round `.get` + once-per-round dedup read + create.
  - postClubNote: create.
  Only unguarded awaits left = `auth.me()` (proven fast) + `req.json()` (local). Audited: zero un-guarded `sb.entities`/`asServiceRole.entities` awaits remain. deno check green on all 5.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** REDEPLOY the 5 functions, then call createCommunityPost **directly** (fetch, valid author_hash) → require **<3s + 200 + post id**, then UI post + comment persist on reload. Plus the full write sweep (echo/pill/witness). Once green, remove createPostDiag.
- **Open Q for later (non-blocking):** WHY the `-created_date` filter hangs on Base44 (likely an unindexed-sort issue — CommunityPost has no created_date index). Guard makes it moot for posting; if posts consistently take ~2.5s, the read is hanging every time and we should drop/replace that sorted rate query.

## 🛑→✅ APP-WIDE WRITE-LOCK ROOT CAUSE FIXED (2026-06-12) — `8d4f79f` · CONFIRMED live (createPostDiag all ok:true) — RLS no longer the blocker
**createPostDiag gave the decisive answer:** `required-only` CommunityPost.create AND the **Echo.create control** BOTH returned **"Permission denied for create operation"** (~250-400ms, fast deny — not a hang, not a field, not moderation).
- **ROOT CAUSE (app-wide):** the B3 audit added a write-lock rls `user_condition: { role: "system" }` to **22 entities**. **asServiceRole does NOT satisfy `role:system`** → EVERY service-role create/update/delete was DENIED. Posting, echoes, comments, reactions, QOTD, witness, twin, clubs, **kindness pool**, wisdom — every write blocked. Missed because we only ever verified reads(200)+function-registration(400), **never a real end-to-end write**.
- **FIX (`8d4f79f`):** replaced every `"role": "system"` → `"role": "admin"` across all 22 locked entities (create/update/delete + read on the 8 private peer entities). **Why admin:** SDK docs — asServiceRole = "admin-level permissions, any data available to the app's admin"; the diag proved it is SUBJECT to rls (denied, not bypassed) so it's evaluated as `role:"admin"`. So `{role:"admin"}` PASSES for the service and still BLOCKS a normal client (`role:"user"`) → **B3 direct-client-write block preserved.** Strictly safe: a client is denied under BOTH system and admin (access never loosens); only the service goes denied→allowed. 14 lurkable-room entities keep public read; 8 peer entities keep read locked (now service-readable). All 22 JSONC parse-validated.
- **Entities:** BookClubPick, CircleMembership, Club, ClubCheckpoint, ClubMembership, ClubNote, Comment, CommunityPost, Echo, GameResponse, GameRound, GoalContribution, IngestErrorLog, QotdResponse, Reaction, RitualContribution, TwinEntry, TwinPair, VoiceNote, WisdomEntry, WitnessRequest, WitnessStrike.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** (1) **SYNC/redeploy the ENTITIES** (schema change, not a function/bundle deploy). (2) **Re-run `createPostDiag`** → expect CommunityPost step **AND** Echo control both `ok:true`. (3) Full live write sweep: real UI **post + comment + echo + kindness-pill + witness send** — all must persist. (4) Once green, **remove createPostDiag** (temporary probe) + re-confirm. Memory: [[base44-service-role-rls-admin]].
- **Note:** all the earlier posting fixes still stand and matter (publish-then-screen `65bf98a` removed the moderation hang; timeout-guard `7877fdb` ensures no create can wedge; resilient create + client error states). This RLS fix is the missing root cause underneath them — writes literally could not persist until now.

## 🔬 COMMUNITYPOST CREATE HANG — ISOLATED + DIAG PROBE (2026-06-12) — `7877fdb` · diag did its job → root cause was RLS (see above)
Live test of `a6b16ab`: createCommunityPost with a valid author_hash **HANGS indefinitely** (abort never fired, promise never settled 8s+); proxy gives up ~17-19s; client error-state works but the post never saves. Reads work; **postEcho's asServiceRole Echo.create works**. So the hang is isolated to the **CommunityPost create** specifically.
- **RLS ruled out:** Echo and CommunityPost have **byte-identical** write locks (`create/update/delete: user_condition role:system`, read omitted). postEcho writes Echo via asServiceRole fine → asServiceRole satisfies role:system equally. Required fields satisfied; postEcho even sends undefined fields. So it's something **specific to the CommunityPost entity/create**, not RLS shape or undefined fields.
- **Why the prior fallback didn't help (`5aec8ab`):** it used `.catch()`, which only catches a THROW. A **hang** never settles → `.catch` never fires → `await` hangs forever → fallback never ran. Fixed: the create is now wrapped in **`withTimeout()` (6s race)** so a hang becomes a catchable rejection → the function always returns (with real `detail`) AND the core-fallback can actually run. (If the full payload hangs on an extra field but core works, the post now publishes in ~6s.)
- **NEW `createPostDiag` (TEMPORARY probe, `7877fdb`):** one fast call that tries **incremental** CommunityPost creates (required-only → +comments_mode → +by → +hidden → +report_count → +flagged → +circle → +club), each timeout-guarded, **stopping at the first failure to NAME the offending field**, plus an **Echo.create control** in the same call (Echo should succeed, CommunityPost should fail — side by side). Cleans up its rows. Returns exact per-step `{ok, ms, error}`.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI — 2 steps:** (1) **DEPLOY** `createCommunityPost` + the NEW `createPostDiag`. (2) **Call `createPostDiag` once** (POST `/functions/createPostDiag`, body `{}` or `{author_hash:"qa_test_hash_888"}`) and paste the JSON back. That pinpoints the cause in one fast call: e.g. `required-only` fails → entity/RLS/migration; `+flagged` fails → that field isn't on the deployed entity → **redeploy the CommunityPost entity schema**. Then remove createPostDiag.

## 🩹 CREATE-500 + UI-HANG FIX (2026-06-12) — `5aec8ab` · superseded by `7877fdb` (hang ≠ throw; timeout-guard added)
Live test of `430dbde` found: hang gone ✓ but createCommunityPost returns **500 `Write failed`** in ~2s (the entity CREATE throws), and the UI still hangs ~17s.
- **Diagnosis:** the create object is identical to pre-re-arch for a clean post (`flagged:false` either way) → the re-arch didn't cause it; the moderation hang was **masking a pre-existing create failure**. Most likely an **optional field (e.g. `flagged`, added in M2) whose entity-schema addition was never deployed** → `create({...flagged...})` rejected. The ~17s UI hang is NOT SDK retries (base44 SDK = plain axios, no retry) — it's the deployed **function-proxy stalling on the 500** before surfacing it (direct curl hits the function endpoint directly = fast 500). axios rejects on 500 so the client catch ran but `onPosted` never did → composer stayed open, no error. **All downstream of the 500.**
- **Fix (`5aec8ab`):** createCommunityPost + addComment build the payload WITHOUT undefined keys and do a **two-step create — full payload, then a CORE-fields-only fallback** (still `hidden:false` → visible) if the first throws. For a Lounge post the core payload is all original **M1** fields (room/author_hash/body/comments_mode/by/hidden), which ARE deployed → the post publishes even if `flagged` isn't on the entity. The 500 now returns the real error in a `detail` field for the next live test. Client resilience: a failed post/comment shows an error + unblocks (`setErr`/`setCommentErr`) instead of hanging; screenContent stays truly fire-and-forget.
- **Verified:** deno check green (createCommunityPost, addComment); vite build exit 0 + eslint 0 errors.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** REDEPLOY the 2 edited functions (+ screenContent if not yet), re-drive a live Lounge **post** + a **comment**: expect 200 in ~1s, composer closes, post appears + persists on reload. If a 500 still occurs, the response `detail` names the exact field/cause → likely action: **redeploy the CommunityPost/Comment ENTITY schemas** so `flagged` (and the other M2 fields) exist (clean long-term fix; the fallback is the safety net).

## 🏗️ POSTING RE-ARCHITECTED → PUBLISH-THEN-SCREEN (2026-06-12) — `65bf98a` · superseded create path by `5aec8ab`; screenContent still pending deploy
The 4s AbortController (`128d891`) did **not** reliably abort the deployed moderation fetch — Halli's live test (valid author_hash) still hung 45s+. Plus Halli's product call: **don't block posting on any API; don't run paid AI per post.** So the fix is structural — remove EVERY synchronous external call from the write paths.
- **Write paths now LOCAL-ONLY (`65bf98a`):** `createCommunityPost`, `addComment`, `postClubNote`, `closeTheWeek`, `submitGameResponse` run only fast local checks — crisis-regex intercept + keyword floor, **no network** — then create + return (<1.5s target). No awaited external call remains on any write path, so the hang cannot recur regardless of why the abort misfired. (`answerQotd` was already local-only.)
- **NEW `screenContent` function:** ASYNC post-publish OpenAI moderation. The **client fires it fire-and-forget** right after a successful create (never awaited). If flagged harmful it pulls the content within moments — post → `hidden:true` (drops from every room, all read `hidden:false`); comment → `status:'removed'` + body withheld (tombstone); borderline → `flagged:true`. Health/reproductive language allowlisted. **Fails open** (leaves content as-is) if the key is missing/errors/times out (8s) — the local floor already passed it. Wired in `Community.jsx` for posts (line 298) + comments (line 204). Posts+comments are screened; club-notes/close-week/game answers (short, aggregate-revealed) rely on the local floor.
- **`jessSupport` sampling:** added deterministic ~1-in-3 sampling on eligible heavy threads → Jess replies are occasional, not per-post → far fewer paid LLM calls. Still async/off the write path (fires on comment-view, gated once-per-post); 12s race retained.
- **Verified:** `deno check` green on all **seven** functions (temp SDK stub); `vite build` exit 0 + `eslint` 0 errors (3 pre-existing warnings, none new). No dangling refs to the removed `moderate`/`openaiModerate`/`openaiFlaggedHarmful` helpers.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI — REDEPLOY:** the 6 edited functions **AND register the NEW `screenContent` function**, then re-drive a live Lounge post + a comment end-to-end (write → Post → 200 in ~1s AND it appears). Record outcome + live state here. NOTE: if screenContent isn't deployed, posting still works fine (the fire-and-forget call just 404s and is swallowed) — screening simply won't run until it's registered.
- Supersedes the `128d891` timeout approach (kept in history). Memory: [[base44-fetch-no-timeout-hangs]].

## 🚨 POSTING HANG — timeout attempt (2026-06-12) — `128d891` · SUPERSEDED by `65bf98a` (publish-then-screen)
**CRITICAL bug Halli found live:** a real Lounge post fired `POST /functions/createCommunityPost` but the request stayed **pending 50s+ and never resolved** — post never saved, UI did nothing, no console error.
- **Root cause (confirmed by reading the code):** the M2 moderation helper `openaiModerate()` did `fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/moderations', …)` with **NO timeout**. A slow/unresponsive OpenAI endpoint → the fetch never settles → the whole function hangs forever. The existing keyword-fallback `catch` never ran because an unresponsive fetch never throws.
- **Fix (`128d891`):** added an **AbortController 4s hard timeout** to EVERY OpenAI moderation fetch on the write paths — `createCommunityPost`, `addComment`, `closeTheWeek`, `submitGameResponse`. On timeout the fetch aborts → throws → the existing **keyword crisis/scrub floor runs and the post STILL goes through** (fail-safe, never blocks). `jessSupport`'s `InvokeLLM` (no abort signal) is raced against a **12s** timeout. Functions now return ~1s (clean) / ≤4s (moderation hung). `answerQotd` + `collectivePool` audited — no external calls, already safe. Note: Deno.serve already returns 500 on any throw, so the unresolved fetch was the *only* hang vector.
- **Verified:** `deno check` green on all five (temp SDK stub + import map). Client unchanged — the composer's success path already calls `onPosted()` (refetch) and `finally` clears `busy`; it only ever hung because the request never returned.
- **⚠️ NEEDS HALLI:** **REDEPLOY the functions** (not just the bundle — this is server-function code), then re-drive a live Lounge post end-to-end (write → Post → request returns 200 in ~1–3s AND the post appears). Record outcome + live bundle/function state here per the baton rule.
